# Feedback source coverage

**19 September 2026:** The counts and “active” labels below belong to the preserved historical inventory. The user's narrower working scope and refreshed source coverage are in [feedback since the last update](feedback-since-last-update.md). Do not treat every pre-release source below as unfinished goal work. Its requirements may remain relevant as regression evidence for an included report.

Current inventory: **569 primary sources**, **717 source/workstream mappings**, **694 active acceptance references**. C423–C426 add **13 defined subjects** on 8 September 2026. The following original-audit counts and initial verification states remain historical; [the addition and acceptance plan](vorshlumpf-feedback-2026-09-08.md) and final section below govern the new scope. Focused plans now contain bounded source, control and gameplay progress. Every full added criterion remains open. Coverage was rechecked on13 September without adding duplicate sources.

On 13 September the first register reconciliation covered all 39 active C423–C426 parent/child references. Each records its stage, evidence-plan link and remaining work under `observed_progress_evidence`; all remain `in-progress` with empty full-completion evidence. The earlier exact R1 evidence for `AC-L36-C424-S01` and its parent is retained. That first pass preserved all 655 older active records and passed the unchanged inventory checker.

A second reviewed pass adds progress to exactly six older references: `AC-L01-GH258`, `AC-L01-T07-R06`, `AC-L01-C390-S06`, `AC-L04-GH256`, `AC-L02-T01-OP` and `AC-L02-GH270`. Each preserves its original scope and completion obligations while naming exact historical results and unfinished work. All 688 other criteria, all 569 source records, retired aliases and other top-level objects are unchanged in that pass. There are now 45 references with progress recorded and 649 whose older labels still require reconciliation. No full criterion is completed. The [current status page](current-status.md) distinguishes completed milestones from full requirements and retains the wider reconciliation task.

This inventory preserves **565 primary entries**: the starting ledger's **564**, plus append-only **C422**, the maintainer acknowledgement discovered at the goal-start refresh. Cutoff: **2026-09-06 23:01:30.701884 UTC** (**7 September 2026, 01:01:30 CEST**).

The machine-readable inventory is [feedback-sources.json](feedback-sources.json). Its initial ledger snapshot had SHA-256 `c70338c45b136fbff309f4bbe28926d92d284076a1a518d8deed2ea7d399a23f`; the [current ledger](../feedback-ledger-2026-09-07.md) now includes the append-only additions. Original source IDs, paraphrases and mappings remain preserved.

**Source-audit corrections are applied and await independent re-review.** The [independent findings](source-audit-findings.md) exposed subjects omitted by short paraphrases. Original paraphrases, provenance and original mappings remain intact; separately labelled supplemental subjects now preserve those obligations. This is inventory work: no technical acceptance criterion or runtime behavior has been verified.

## Schema and interpretation

- `original_paraphrase`, `provenance` and `original_ledger_ids` preserve the baseline capture. `ledger_ids` is the union of original and explicit supplemental mappings; `supplemental_ledger_ids` makes every added mapping visible.
- `supplemental_subjects` stores separately auditable subject text, qualifications, related-source links, raw-capture body hashes and pending child acceptance references. The source's original paraphrase is never silently expanded or replaced.
- `AC-Lxx-<source-id>` is the source/workstream obligation. `AC-Lxx-<source-id>-Snn` is a supplemental-subject child, preserving several distinct requests or claims within one source and workstream. Parent/child obligations are navigation and completion obligations, not counts of independent defects or completed tests.
- `initial_disposition`, `verification_status` and `classification_review_status` remain separate. `classification_history` records the three corrected classifications. `retired_acceptance_criteria` preserves C307's former ID without keeping a made-up implementation task active.
- `workstreams` holds reverse source, subject and acceptance mappings plus historical evidence. `relationships` preserves crossposts and provisional antecedents without collapsing reports.

All measurable acceptance definitions and required evidence still need focused investigation. Raw-body reading establishes the source's meaning; it does not establish its causal diagnosis, historical fix success, shipped behavior or final-build correctness. Linked external logs, saves and videos remain evidence leads unless their inspection is separately recorded.

`P` means pending investigation and unverified; `C` means context only with no technical verification applicable; `U` means the source body is unavailable. Supplemental subject text appears above and in JSON. All baseline paraphrases below are preserved exactly, with formatting escapes only.

## Consistency checks and remaining review

[check-feedback-inventory.py](check-feedback-inventory.py) checks the current corpus, additions, captured identities/timestamps, plan definitions and cross-references without writes. Run `python docs/plans/check-feedback-inventory.py`. The historical [correction script](apply-source-audit-corrections.py) also delegates `--check` to this checker when additions are present; its old `--apply` transformation refuses to overwrite an extended corpus. Neither command establishes implementation or gameplay acceptance.

Checks confirm 565 unique IDs/URLs/native identity pairs, exact source-type counts, all 564 baseline paraphrases and original mappings, all 55 workstreams, explicit supplemental source/subject/acceptance mappings and their reverse maps, C307's traceable retirement, two hidden bodies, and zero observed implementation evidence. Parent/child criteria remain pending definition. The earlier identity audit also matched the refreshed Steam/GitHub captures and source timestamps.

These checks establish inventory consistency. **The corrected classifications and supplemental interpretations await independent re-review.** Each technical or requested subject still requires focused investigation, measurable acceptance criteria, implementation where warranted and actual behavior verification. No ordinary-bill-gather criteria or other implementation plan was changed by this correction.

## Robot mapping correction and alias interpretation

Correction `robot-source-mapping-correction-2026-09-08` removes C424's false L06 hospital association. The register retains full prior records in `retired_acceptance_criteria` with `retirement_status=superseded-mapping-correction` and direct `replacement_acceptance_id` links. These two aliases are distinct from C307's one nonactionable retirement. Current totals are 694 active acceptance references and 3 retained historical records; all 13 added subjects and the complete manual command criterion remain pending.

`mapping_corrections` records the reason and evidence; C424 and its addition record link back to it. L08/C002 remains a distinguishing comparison, without a new C424 acceptance mapping: explicit commands work in that report while ordinary/automatic activation fails. The original 565 source objects, 655 acceptance objects and raw C423–C426 supplement are unchanged.
